# PacEdu English Subject Box — ECE Printable Daily Worksheets (Days 1–115)

**Status:** ORIGINAL PACEDU ECE WORKSHEET DRAFT — `PENDING_TEACHER_REVIEW` and `PENDING_OFFICIAL_INDICATOR_VERIFICATION`. This is not an official Fiji Ministry worksheet pack. A Fiji-qualified ECE/English educator must verify age suitability, current authorised outcomes and local practice before learner release.

## Daily printing and date rules
- One learning day has four separate print versions: Copper, Bronze, Silver and Gold. Print only the learner's current pathway; all four versions share the same learning intention.
- **Country:** `{{PACEDU_COUNTRY_NAME}}` · **School time zone:** `{{PACEDU_IANA_TIME_ZONE}}` · **Local print date:** `{{PACEDU_LOCAL_DATE}}` · **Print time:** `{{PACEDU_LOCAL_DATETIME}}`.
- The print viewer supplies the local date using the school's configured IANA time zone. If the school's time zone is not configured for the selected country, it displays a warning and clearly labels the device time zone as a fallback.
- The 115 learning days follow the school's approved calendar. Weekends, holidays, term breaks and make-up days are not inferred from the date and do not automatically advance the learning sequence.

## Learner and teacher details
Learner: ____________________  School: ____________________  ECE group: __________
Teacher: ____________________  Ability pathway: ____________________
Official ECE outcome/indicator and exact authorised source edition/page: **PENDING_VERIFICATION**

## ECE worksheet approach
- These are play-based response sheets, not formal written tests. A child may respond through speech, home language, sign, gesture, pointing, object choice, AAC, movement, drawing, mark-making or adult-scribed words.
- Use real objects and teacher-drawn or school-approved pictures where useful. Do not require children to read printed instructions independently.
- No forced speech, timed work, formal grammar drills or fixed numeric marks. Record the prompt, support and communication mode.
- Do not assume a particular family structure, culture, home language or ability. Respect the child's choice to observe before joining.
- Copper, Bronze, Silver and Gold are flexible access/challenge pathways, not permanent labels.

---

# Day 1 — Term 1, Week 1, Day 1 — Copper Worksheet

**Weekly focus:** My name, identity and belonging  
**Shared learning intention:** Recognise and communicate own name; listen to and respond to familiar names.  
**Activity:** Find my name  
**Materials:** Name cards, mirror, family-approved identity words  
**Country:** {{PACEDU_COUNTRY_NAME}} · **School time zone:** {{PACEDU_IANA_TIME_ZONE}} · **Local print date:** {{PACEDU_LOCAL_DATE}}

**Copper learner support**
The adult models first and offers one clear object or picture at a time. Use two choices, tactile or large-print access, gesture/sign/AAC, movement and generous wait time. The child may point, match, move, vocalise, draw or choose; adult may scribe.

**Teacher invitation**
Place three name/photo cards in front of the child. Invite the child to find their own card by looking, pointing, touching, matching or saying their name.

**Try it**
Find your name card. Match it to your place card. Show one thing you like using an object, picture, gesture or word.

**My choice / response**
I chose / showed / made / matched:

____________________________________________________________________________

____________________________________________________________________________

**Draw, make a mark, attach a picture or record the child's selected response:**





**Teacher observation (record what the child did, not a generic score)**
- What the child communicated or demonstrated: __________________________________
- Prompt/support provided and communication mode: ______________________________
- Evidence of the shared learning intention: ____________________________________
- Next small step: _____________________________________________________________
- Official outcome verified? YES / NO. If YES, record exact source and page: __________

**Teacher guidance:** Accept a meaningful response through the child's accessible communication mode. Record the actual response and the support used. Do not mark speech, handwriting or letter knowledge unless it is the verified focus of this activity.

**Teacher initials:** __________  **Learner date (school local date):** __________________

# Day 2 — Term 1, Week 1, Day 2 — Copper Worksheet

**Weekly focus:** My name, identity and belonging  
**Shared learning intention:** Recognise and communicate own name; listen to and respond to familiar names.  
**Activity:** My name has a sound  
**Materials:** Name cards, mirror, family-approved identity words  
**Country:** {{PACEDU_COUNTRY_NAME}} · **School time zone:** {{PACEDU_IANA_TIME_ZONE}} · **Local print date:** {{PACEDU_LOCAL_DATE}}

**Copper learner support**
The adult models first and offers one clear object or picture at a time. Use two choices, tactile or large-print access, gesture/sign/AAC, movement and generous wait time. The child may point, match, move, vocalise, draw or choose; adult may scribe.

**Teacher invitation**
Say each child's name naturally and invite the child to notice its sound or rhythm. Do not demand a particular pronunciation.

**Try it**
Listen when your name is said. Choose your name card from two cards. Tap or move once for each part of your name if you wish.

**My choice / response**
I chose / showed / made / matched:

____________________________________________________________________________

# Day 4 — Term 1, Week 1, Day 4 — Copper Worksheet

**Weekly focus:** My name, identity and belonging  
**Shared learning intention:** Recognise and communicate own name; listen to and respond to familiar names.  
**Activity:** A picture of me  
**Materials:** Name cards, mirror, family-approved identity words  
**Country:** {{PACEDU_COUNTRY_NAME}} · **School time zone:** {{PACEDU_IANA_TIME_ZONE}} · **Local print date:** {{PACEDU_LOCAL_DATE}}

**Copper learner support**
The adult models first and offers one clear object or picture at a time. Use two choices, tactile or large-print access, gesture/sign/AAC, movement and generous wait time. The child may point, match, move, vocalise, draw or choose; adult may scribe.

**Teacher invitation**
Offer a mirror, paper and drawing tools. Model drawing a face or a chosen self-representation; children may use collage or adult scribing.

**Try it**
Make a picture that represents you. Choose a colour, shape or object to add. Show what your picture means.

**Weekly focus:** Greetings and friendly conversations  
**Shared learning intention:** Use greetings, names, turn-taking and simple responses in familiar interactions.  
**Activity:** Greeting circle

**Country:** {{PACEDU_COUNTRY_NAME}} · **School time zone:** {{PACEDU_IANA_TIME_ZONE}} · **Local print date:** {{PACEDU_LOCAL_DATE}}

**Copper learner support**
The adult models first and offers one clear object or picture at a time. Use two choices, tactile or large-print access, gesture/sign/AAC, movement and generous wait time. The child may point, match, move, vocalise, draw or choose; adult may scribe.

**Teacher invitation**
Practise greeting a puppet, teacher or partner in a small group. Respect a child's choice not to make physical contact.

# Day 11 — Term 1, Week 3, Day 1 — Copper Worksheet

**Weekly focus:** Listening to familiar sounds  
**Shared learning intention:** Attend to environmental sounds and distinguish familiar sound sources.  
**Activity:** Sound walk  
**Materials:** Sound walk, safe sound objects, quiet/loud listening  
**Country:** {{PACEDU_COUNTRY_NAME}} · **School time zone:** {{PACEDU_IANA_TIME_ZONE}} · **Local print date:** {{PACEDU_LOCAL_DATE}}

**Copper learner support**
The adult models first and offers one clear object or picture at a time. Use two choices, tactile or large-print access, gesture/sign/AAC, movement and generous wait time. The child may point, match, move, vocalise, draw or choose; adult may scribe.

**Teacher invitation**
Listen to safe nearby sounds indoors or outdoors. Pause between sounds and let children point or move toward the source.

**Weekly focus:** Naming and describing familiar objects  
**Shared learning intention:** Use familiar nouns and describing words to communicate meaning.

**Copper learner support**
The adult models first and offers one clear object or picture at a time. Use two choices, tactile or large-print access, gesture/sign/AAC, movement and generous wait time. The child may point, match, move, vocalise, draw or choose; adult may scribe.

**Teacher invitation**
Offer safe objects that can be sorted by one visible feature such as colour, size or use.

## Final release gate
- [ ] 115 learning days × four separate ability-group print versions = 460 ECE worksheets.
- [ ] Fiji-qualified ECE/English educator reviews every weekly sequence and the daily tasks.
- [ ] Current authorised Fiji ECE outcomes/indicators and source edition/page verified; no codes invented.
- [ ] School country, IANA time zone and approved calendar configured.
- [ ] Android Chrome print preview confirms one selected worksheet per print page.
- [ ] Child privacy, consent and safe use of photos/work samples checked.
- [ ] Student release remains blocked until review and approval are recorded.
